# Local acts (decisions of local and county councils): `local/bucuresti/cgmb/`, 2019

734 acts, 734 versions. Each act lives in a folder named by its official identifier, `<kind>/<year>/<number>/` (Legea nr. 31/1990 is `lege/1990/31/`); its README.md lists its articles and the decisions made by hand. Dates are in-force days. Generated on every publish; the same list is in [2019.json](2019.json).
